ALEXANDRI NECKAM DE NATURIS RERUM LIBRI DUO
Alexander Neckam (1157-1217)
Edited by Thomas Wright, Rolls Series, London, 1863
========================================================================
PROVENANCE NOTE (Tower of Babel staging): Latin text after Thomas Wright's
1863 Rolls Series edition (public domain scan, Google/Internet Archive). The
work is complete: Liber Primus and Liber Secundus of Neckam's "De naturis
rerum" (on the natures of things), the great 12th-century English natural
history with its marvels. Wright's appended poem "De laudibus divinae
sapientiae" is not included (separate work).
========================================================================
